# Harvest lessons audit - 2026-10-06

Audit for FR #2705 (Simon, 13:07 BST on 2026-10-06: "verify ALL lessons landed"). Every `Lessons:` bullet ever submitted through intake:

- 1234 via-intake harvest issues (closed),
- 29 via-intake harvest draft PRs (21 closed, 8 open),
- 212 held rows in ionos `intake/outbox-hold-2583` (2 of the 214 held rows had no lessons),
- 0 in agentic_fomprep (no via-intake PRs or issues there). The 2 lessons that mention fomprep are fleet-process rules (UAT host pin, needs-mrb1), so they route to bobiverse books.

There are also 24 ionos `receipt_recorded` records, which keep the title only. Their lesson bodies were dropped by the old receipt path; that is the bug fixed by FR #2705. 4 of them were recovered from MarchHare seat session logs and are listed below; the other 20 are unrecoverable.

**Landed** = present on `main` in a SKILL.md, matched by citation of the source harvest number or by TF-IDF similarity >= 0.22 to a SKILL.md bullet (method: lessons audit script). Missing lessons are generalised into the `## Harvest digest (lessons audit 2026-10-06)` section of the target book in the linked PR.
